<a id="introduction"></a>
## 🍌 Introduction

**Explore Gemini 4 Neon through public workflows, visual prototypes, integrations, and evaluations. Each case links to its source and describes the model's role and the reported result.**

<a id="overview"></a>
## 📊 Overview

**242 Gemini 4 Neon cases** with 309 source visuals.

- Engineering and Integrations · 22
- Graphics and Interface Prototypes · 141
- Games and Simulations · 19
- Knowledge Work · 20
- Evaluations and Limits · 40

> [!NOTE]
> Each case links to its source. Treat reported timings, costs, and benchmarks as observations from the stated test conditions.

<a id="quick-start"></a>
## ⚡ Quick Start

1. Choose a case from the menu and open its original source.
2. Check the model role, tool, settings and outcome that the source reports.
3. Compare cases with matching tasks and settings. Treat creator-reported times and costs as observations from those runs.

<a id="case-1"></a>
### Case 1: [Observe instability during unattended runs](https://x.com/IndependentEco/status/2123155946801070204) (by [@IndependentEco](https://x.com/IndependentEco))

**Track retries and completion in unattended runs, and keep a separate record of the supervising model.**

The tester reports launch-day instability when running Neon headlessly and says Opus 5.5 monitored the run.

The screenshots document that environment. The report does not attribute the root cause.

### Case 96: [Animate a stick-figure battle in Three.js](https://x.com/chetaslua/status/2123720700670398680) (by [@chetaslua](https://x.com/chetaslua))

**Test whether a coded fight sequence stays readable across camera moves and scene changes.**

<a id="case-82"></a>
### Case 82: [Rank printable 3D figures in Wojak-Bench](https://x.com/lukaspet/status/2123169317076574513) (by [@lukaspet](https://x.com/lukaspet))

**Read the posted placement as a snapshot of the pairwise comparison, and check the current board before citing a rating.**

The benchmark coauthor reported that Neon debuted in fourth place. The posted leaderboard snapshot gives Neon a rating of 1056.

Models turn the same reference drawing into printable figures. Visitors compare pairs of outputs, so the live rating can change as votes accumulate.

### Case 194: [Track cache-hit changes in Antigravity](https://x.com/leo114119/status/2124119129863209157) (by [@leo114119](https://x.com/leo114119))

**Compare cache hit rates over time before attributing quota changes to model quality.**

The creator reports a 98.9% cache hit rate in the later post and roughly 50% in an earlier post.

The later attachment displays 98.1% for the current request and 78.7% cumulative. These figures differ from the caption and describe the displayed session.

<a id="case-208"></a>
### Case 208: [Score a figure-eight knot with a physics replay](https://x.com/li9292/status/2124165078014427440) (by [@li9292](https://x.com/li9292))

**Check that a model returns a usable knot description before comparing its physical score to competing outputs.**

The creator's KnotBench v0 replay shows one shared figure-eight-knot task across four models. Neon did not produce an extractable knot description in the displayed run.

The clip records Neon at L0 without entering simulation. Astra and Sol reach L5 in the displayed run. The video identifies this as a single submission per model.

The post shows an 87.4 overall result for Neon in the displayed LiveBench 2.0 release.

The benchmark owner's release files reproduce the 87.4 result with Long Agentic weighted 2× and Coding and Design weighted 1.5×. The ranking reflects the release and evaluation setup shown.
